# S04 for GPT-5.6 Luna xhigh — complete human reference

This file collects the pack for human reading. Do not load it wholesale into every worker. Use one task and its role-specific dispatch. The supplied queue defines one real lesson, not train-until-improved. Runtime commands and readiness must be resolved from the actual checkout. No application, CI or real model run was executed to prepare this artifact.

## File: README.md

# XIIGEN S04 · GPT-5.6 Luna xhigh

## Deliverable
15 task briefs, 60 role-specific prompts, one owner-adopted bounded real-lesson grant, source/skill context, test/effect observations, finite recovery rules and final evidence/S05 handoff. This is a preparation artifact. No repository implementation, CI or model-training work was performed while creating it.

The uploaded queue defines S04 as one real CodeDocumentation lesson with an accepted compatible model, isolated fixed evaluation, complete state and explicit training authority. One honest improve/revert/unchanged result is required; improvement must not be fabricated. The package retains this scope and the broader S05–S09 program.

## Install
Review the archive outside the checkout. Merge only `overlay/.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/` into the existing matching directory. Do not overwrite conflicting current files; compare and preserve local additions. No installer runs automatically. The package does not write AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, original skills, S03, active.md, state.json, code or model files.

Choose GPT-5.6 Luna / xhigh in native Kiro controls, then adopt START-PROMPT.md once. That grants the bounded local real lesson and ordinary engineering/retry/CI work without middle user approvals. Existing total USD20 CI budget is shared and not renewed. Model/download/cloud expenses and remote real-artifact publication are not silently added.

## Read order
Manager: START-PROMPT, AUTHORIZATION, RESUME, ROADMAP, RUN-CONTRACT, OPERATING-POLICY. Read CI/RECOVERY playbooks only when applicable. Worker: its one task and role dispatch, actual adopted grant, relevant current skills/source and prior result. The combined all-tasks file is for human browsing, not every worker's context.

## Important current-source facts
Source pin `15f2f2cb905f3e8db37103bc032678b789071f6e`; planning `f8a0bde3c596422f349bf7b87093394c8a7bdc44`. Baseline-before-pair order, detailed negative measurement retention, one actual optimizer step and the real entrypoint must be resolved against post-S03 code. Do not assume a proposed class or command already exists. `context-manifest.json` labels uninspected definitions and partial windows explicitly. The five core skills were read in kickoff ranges, not the entire dependency library.

## Completion
Engineering tests and actual live run evidence are separate. The user-adopted grant lets the manager automatically pass internal readiness gates and perform the lesson, not skip missing model/data/host requirements. An absent permitted host or private data cannot be repaired by fabricating evidence. Valid negative quality is allowed; invalid/incomplete evaluation is not unchanged. No second update after applied/uncertain state. Retain the exact prior full state or guarded child and prove fresh load.

See VALIDATION.md for the packaging checks actually performed. They do not establish application correctness, independent review, actual model behavior or runtime Luna performance.

## File: AUTHORIZATION.md

# S04 — one adoption, bounded engineering and one real lesson

This is the authorization text the owner adopts with START-PROMPT.md. The package itself neither runs commands nor proves prior acceptance. Once adopted, no further human confirmation is needed for the operations explicitly covered here. A newer direct owner instruction, real tool restriction or mandatory external protection still applies.

## Scope and effect
Complete S04.00–S04.14: preserve accepted work, close actual integration gaps, test them, run one honest CodeDocumentation lesson using the already approved local open-weight teacher/evaluator, record the outcome, and prepare S05. This does not mean train until the score improves. An unchanged or worse measured result can complete the experiment correctly when the original state is retained/restored and the evidence is complete.

I extend the adopted S03 v2 Luna/xhigh, role-scoped reading, coverage-driven internal plan/code review and correction-loop method to S04. This explicitly replaces inherited GPT-5.5 defaults, fixed 30/50-pass quotas, blanket reloads after unchanged status commits, and per-operation human-approval wording for this batch. It does not edit AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md or the original skills or weaken product requirements.

I authorize required source/test/fixture and thin operational integration changes; build/discovery/focused/impacted verification; ordinary candidate commits/pushes in codex/dpo-implementation; bounded CI workflow repairs, dispatch/rerun/cancel/force-cancel; and necessary CI-only PRs into main subject to protections. The architect and independent plan reviewer determine a concrete minimal edit set without asking me about each method. The manager performs routine GitHub/state operations without four new workers for each API call. Source authors never independently accept their own results.

## Explicit real-lesson grant
This S04 work order supplies the previously missing real-training authorization, only after its internal readiness checks pass. It authorizes:
- Read the actual already approved local CodeDocumentation parent weights, compatible tokenizer/configuration and complete prior optimizer/RNG state.
- Invoke the already approved locally hosted open-weight Qwen authoring and evaluation endpoints in their existing roles. No new provider/model choice, cloud API purchase or external credentials search.
- Obtain at most one separate teaching-task student answer under the fixed admitted generation settings when no compatible saved teaching answer exists; preserve the required model state and do not use closed-case text.
- Measure one baseline under the frozen S03 diagnostic contract; store and reopen it; author and seal at most one new pair only when required; apply at most one actual optimizer update to that pair; measure the resulting trial under the same contract.
- Create this lesson's immutable local artifacts inside the repository's actual canonical model/attempt/dataset/evaluation layout, preserving all existing accepted artifacts. These durable results are NOT disposable scratch.
- Let the existing quality gate promote a valid improved child locally, or retain/restore the complete previous accepted state otherwise. No human approval is needed between those pre-authorized alternatives. The guard and separate final evidence review remain mandatory.
- Resume a completed update by validating its saved complete bundle and evaluating only. No new baseline, pair, update, experiment identity or source reset may hide a retry.

This grant also covers the existing typed initial-optimizer/RNG authorization for a genuinely first-ever DPO lesson on an already accepted pretrained parent, ONLY when source/lineage evidence proves that no previous mutable DPO state is expected and the existing registered seed/initialization policy is fixed before measurement. The internal reviewers validate that condition; another human approval is not required. It does not allow replacing lost/corrupt prior state, creating a new random student, inventing historical optimizer counters or selecting a different seed after seeing quality. Record and persist the initial state before mutation.

At most one new sealed pair and one new weight update belong to this objective across sessions, workers, commits and runtime restarts. If a valid unfinished attempt is already bound to the objective, it occupies the same slot. A completed valid negative comparison closes that lesson; do not rerun it to seek a more favorable score.

If the baseline already satisfies the existing noise-aware target, do NOT train or create an unnecessary pair. Record `no_training_needed` and hand off to the accepted S03 certification route. That is a valid no-op disposition, not proof that S04's one-update experiment occurred and not an unseen certificate. No new real certification exposure is authorized by this one-lesson grant.

## Bounds (proposed operational defaults, not source claims)
Use the more restrictive of existing approved settings and these ceilings. Resolve exact values in RUN-CONTRACT.md before any real call; do not lower mandatory case/repeat counts to fit a cap.
- One real lesson slot; one pair; one optimizer update. No hyperparameter search, batch expansion or extra accepted-pair rehearsal updates.
- Authoring: at most three dispatched requests, only to recover transport or invalid protocol output before any pair is sealed. Stop after the first valid admissible result; no ranking several valid candidates by exam scores. Log every attempt.
- Diagnostic baseline: one complete series. Trial: one complete series, plus at most one replacement series ONLY after the earlier trial series was incomplete/invalid for a recorded protocol/transport reason. A complete valid comparison is never discarded. A baseline retry before any pair/update is allowed only for a genuinely incomplete series, at most one replacement, and every call remains charged to the same grant.
- Let N be the immutable case count and R the approved repeat count. The cumulative evaluator ceiling is at most 4*N*R, further limited by the actual configured admission limits. Fresh comparisons still contain exactly N*R baseline + N*R trial results; incomplete history is not folded into valid averages or erased from consumption.
- Four hours cumulative supervised real-runtime allowance for this lesson, including resume attempts; finite current per-call/series timeouts. This is an operational limit, not a predicted duration. At exhaustion leave the state safe and complete offline work; do not reset the clock by restarting.
- No new paid model/API, paid GPU, larger runner, model download, account subscription or certificate purchase. Existing local model service may be started/restarted normally only when its ownership/command/endpoint are established and doing so does not disrupt another session or require privilege changes.

## Workspace, execution and privacy
Local canonical checkout is C:\Projects\llm_mvp_core, branch codex/dpo-implementation. I explicitly preserve the exception to the hard no-sibling/no-copy/no-worktree/no-quarantine-fallback rule solely for ephemeral GITHUB_WORKSPACE of authorized CI jobs. Record its actual path. No new local clone/worktree/quarantine. Preserve existing staged/unstaged/untracked work, especially the S02 final-evidence file.

CI is authorized for code tests with synthetic or already approved test data. It is NOT proof that the local model/data/server exists, and is NOT automatically authorized to receive private corpora, paid references, real pairs, checkpoints or training logs containing their text. Execute the real lesson only on the established policy-permitted local/previously approved private runtime with those assets. Never bypass SAC/WDAC, firewall, sandbox restrictions, credentials or license guards to complete the run. Determine real-host feasibility before the live phase, not after changing weights. If no permitted real runtime exists, complete the engineering work and return the exact external blocker without claiming the real experiment complete.

Luna workers receive source code and sanitized evidence, not closed examination content. The isolated approved evaluator receives only what its assessment contract permits; the authoring teacher receives a separate teaching task and approved teaching reference. Credentials and audit salts stay in their existing secure configuration; logs/CLI examples do not print them.

## Shared budget and publication
Carry the REMAINDER of the existing total USD20 GitHub Actions series budget, including compute/storage, into S04. A new package, task or session does not renew it. If this is the first adoption of that series, the total ceiling is USD20. Account for earlier consumed/reserved amounts; unknown free minutes do not require reapproval: estimate conservatively without them. No changing account spending limits or payment methods. One planned computing job at a time, current supported standard images, bounded timeouts and 7–14 day evidence retention. Record reservations in existing state, not a second billing ledger. Existing Kiro subscription limits remain.

Source/config/test and sanitized evidence commits are allowed before or after CI with honest candidate/acceptance labels. Remote publication of actual DPO text, model weights, private training data or exposure ledgers is S05 and requires its existing artifact-specific authority; this S04 grant does not upload them. A private GitHub repo is still remote disclosure. Preserve the distinction between local checkpoint promotion, source-code publication and remote model-artifact publication.

## Human escalation — rare and exact
No new human approval for ordinary scope decisions, worker replacement, fix/rebuild/review, CI cancellation/replacement or the one admitted lesson's phases. Escalate only real missing owner-only input/access, 2FA/SSO, obligatory external approval, budget exhaustion, a new license/product/security choice, or destructive action with uncertain ownership. Collect the exact missing item once, reuse established facts, and continue independent useful work. Do not create another authority-intake document chain or declare missing policy-provider because old scratch cannot be deleted.

## File: RESUME.md

# Resume without losing the existing result

1. Recheck the canonical checkout, branch, current HEAD and local diff; preserve prior work. Read only the current live summary and named result/authority records. The last source snapshot inspected for this package was `15f2f2cb905f3e8db37103bc032678b789071f6e` with a commit message about S03.03 evidence; no complete S03 acceptance is implied.
2. Find any current S04 objective and its actual production session/attempt/measurement artifacts BEFORE allocating another. Reconcile effect state using RECOVERY-PLAYBOOK.md; manager notes are not proof of zero updates.
3. If all S04 engineering already exists, reuse it and its candidate-matched evidence. If S04 real measurement already completed, verify it and proceed to final review. Do not schedule another learning attempt solely because this ZIP is new.
4. If S01/S02/S03 functional prerequisites still have a concrete gap, route to their existing bounded task with inherited engineering authority, preserving this S04 queue. Historical directory holds remain separate; do not falsify upstream acceptance.
5. Load the selected task and role packet. Where context ranges drift, resolve the same symbol in the new code and record current path/line/input identity. Unresolved entry names in context-manifest.json are explicit discovery work, not assumed APIs.
6. Carry remaining shared CI budget, real call/time allowance and the one-update slot. Record actual workers/jobs; replace a missing worker safely and keep a separate final reviewer. No budget/time renewal on session restart.
7. Continue from actual next effect: prepared baseline -> sealed pair -> applied bundle -> full comparison -> selected state -> final evidence. Completed artifacts are reused; incomplete effects follow the finite recovery policy.

## Minimal restart packet (use existing task report)
Current task; objective/run identity; actual source/compiled-input and data/settings identities; selected model; last verified durable phase; last uncertain operation and request/process ID; remaining budgets/time; exact files the next writer may edit; active worker/job ownership; existing raw evidence; next permitted action. Do not paste the full previous transcript or closed examination data.

## File: OPERATING-POLICY.md

# S04 operating policy — internal gates, no middle human approvals

## 1. One live task and real roles
Load the selected task's eight sections, one corresponding dispatch, relevant full method/file context and the actual skill sections. Do not load all 60 role prompts or the combined human reference into each worker. A summary is navigation, not a replacement for the code being edited. Read all of an edited file and the complete affected call chain; read unchanged dependencies only when needed. Record revision/working-diff identities and actual reads.

For a new product boundary: architect proposes exact existing/proposed owners and edit scope; a separate plan reviewer admits the contract; the executor implements/tests; a different code/evidence reviewer checks the final diff and results. These are four roles, not four independent product reviews. Return corrections to the writer, rerun affected checks, and re-review the delta. Reuse an accepted unchanged plan rather than reopening it at each runtime checkpoint.

For the live tasks, S04.08 already admits the complete staged runtime contract. Each later architect/plan-review dispatch performs only a bounded candidate/input/readiness delta check; routine progressing through a granted state is not another design approval and never a request to the user. The manager may cancel/read/dispatch CI, update pointers and commit selected source without another architect per command.

If a worker returns nothing, inspect its status and owned changes. Terminate or revoke its write ownership before replacement. After confirmed unavailable executors, the manager may act as executor under AUTHORIZATION.md, labelled parent-executed. A separate final reviewer is still required; do not write a verdict in another agent's name. Unknown runtime model telemetry is labelled UNVERIFIED, not an endless investigation or a false claim. Request gpt-5.6-luna/xhigh in native controls.

## 2. State, evidence and ordering
Use the existing canonical active.md/state.json and work/S04/<task-id>.md. Preserve history. Keep `implementation`, `test_verification`, `live_execution`, `quality_outcome`, `acceptance`, `publication` separate using existing schema equivalents. The task catalogue is not a live status reset. One objective/run identity is bound durably to one lesson; a local report saying 'not done' never grants another mutation.

S04.00–08 are engineering/readiness tasks. Synthetic tests may perform tiny real arithmetic but are not a real domain experiment. S04.09–13 carry one real lesson through baseline, pair, update, evaluation and reopen. These task boundaries are work/evidence responsibilities, not a requirement for five separate CLI calls. In particular, if the legitimate coordinator applies, evaluates and promotes in one call at S04.11, S04.12 must verify those exact results, not evaluate again. S04.14 is final independent acceptance and S05 handoff.

Never hold a hidden in-memory baseline or optimizer across worker contexts. Use the production durable boundary. A separate session that resumes work must read the same grant, source candidate, artifact manifests, effect receipts and remaining resources; no new budget or pair slot. Stop contradictory concurrent writers; once the real candidate is frozen, do not change its evaluated model/configuration mid-experiment.

## 3. Technical dependencies, not blanket phase flags
Before a real call/update, S01 same-run semantics, S02 complete applicable state recovery, and S03 evaluator/exam/exposure/quality contracts required by this route need current accepted evidence. The last inspected branch commit mentioned S03.03; that is NOT evidence that S03.12 is complete. Resolve the actual live status. A real missing prerequisite becomes its smallest admitted repair/verification task, not duplicate implementation or a false PASS.

Historical cleanup holds remain open but are not by themselves evidence that a separate contract cannot be designed. Do not waive an explicit live acceptance criterion silently. Final S04 real acceptance states exactly which upstream requirements were accepted, any remaining full-phase holds, and why the selected safe route was admitted. No physical deletion of old16/new12, unknown data or caches.

## 4. Decisions without owner interruption
| Condition | Manager/worker action | Must not do |
|---|---|---|
| Equivalent capability and evidence already exist | Map requirements, reuse unchanged code, verify relevant candidate identity | Rebuild it because there is a new task ID |
| Exact API/location unresolved | Resolve current definition/callers in the named module; fix the local source packet | Invent an API or widen to the whole repository history |
| Compiler/test defect | Diagnose first meaningful error, repair minimal owning code/fixture, rebuild/test/review | Ask owner about each line or weaken assertion |
| Additional owning file necessary | Architect supplies delta/invariants; separate plan reviewer admits it internally | Expand product scope or disable protection |
| CI environment issue | CI-PLAYBOOK decision; no new permission loop | Treat one blocked lane as failed product or repeat until green |
| No real model/data/runtime available | Verify configured paths/access once; continue engineering, identify exact missing asset/action | Use TinyTrainingFixtureCL as real domain data or download arbitrary model |
| Real update may already have happened | RECOVERY-PLAYBOOK: classify durable commit state; no new trainer call while uncertain | Reset run ID, reseed, delete claim or retrain |
| Valid complete measurement is negative | Keep/restored parent, persist actual measurements, accept honest experiment procedure if all checks pass | Hyperparameter search or repeated evaluation to force improvement |
| Source or input changed mid-run | Stop mutation, preserve original run, reconcile compatibility; evidence never relabelled | Swap candidate and keep old score |
| Reviewer missing | Replace separate reviewer; preserve pending acceptance while doing independent work | Self-approve |
| Context close/restart | One concise handoff with actual worker/run/artifact IDs, receipts and remaining resources | Claim background execution if no real process/job remains |

## 5. Finish every useful admitted step
Continue automatically to the next dependency-ready task. No final answer merely because a commit, permission read, static review or one task succeeded. Do not inflate progress with repeated diagnostic sections. One short result delta at meaningful transitions; complete source, test and live evidence remains referenced. Human status uses the user's current language; preserve existing Russian product strings/comments without opportunistic translation.

At the end of S04, distinguish: code completed; real attempt executed or not; quality improved/regressed/unchanged/invalid; selected model; independent verdict; local/remote artifacts. A pending external condition does not turn a missing real run into completion. A valid negative quality result is not a product-code bug to fix until scores rise.

## File: RUN-CONTRACT.md

# Resolve the one-lesson execution contract before real side effects

The executor fills the existing work record plus the actual production session contract from verified source/configuration and assets. `templates/run-contract.example.json` is a NON-EXECUTABLE shape with null unresolved values. Never make a null/untrusted field true by assumption. A complete schema is not execution evidence.

## 1. Deterministic resolution (no repeated human choice)
| Input | Resolve automatically from | Rejection/fallback |
|---|---|---|
| Domain/unit | Existing accepted S03 CodeDocumentation target and registered profile; use its exact slug, design pattern and model tree | Multiple conflicting active targets: one exact unresolved input; do not pick the best score or switch domains after measuring |
| Parent checkpoint | Actual PrepareStart/accepted compatible loader with verified numeric lineage and hashes | Missing/corrupt/LFS pointer text is not a parent. Reopen existing authorized local storage. No TinyTrainingFixtureCL/random bootstrap |
| Tokenizer/model/LoRA | Parent runtime-compatibility passport and actual supported construction | No guessed LlmConfig, changed vocab, quantization or rank to make it fit |
| Optimizer/RNG source | Existing complete S02 bundle that matches the selected accepted parent and parameter dimensions | Null is not permission to cold-start. Use the existing supported root-state policy under the adopted S04 grant only when evidence proves the legitimate first-DPO condition |
| Evaluator/teacher | Existing approved local Qwen factories, profile IDs, digests, roles, permits and license/deployment evidence | No cloud substitution, fabricated provenance, operator-decision loop when current records already answer it |
| Diagnostic corpus | Existing sealed S03 diagnostic corpus and provenance, stable case IDs/order/hash | Closed certification corpus must not be substituted for diagnostic/teaching input |
| Teaching input/reference | Existing approved teaching-only pool, selected deterministically by an admitted weakness category, not closed-case text | No missing paid reference fabricated by Luna; if the approved teaching reference is unavailable, report that input rather than generating exam leakage |
| Prior pair examples | Verified accepted teaching pairs as prompt examples only | Exclude them from the optimizer update; record their IDs as context, not the new pair |
| N and R | Actual frozen corpus and existing noise-policy repeat count | Preserve all required cases/repeats; no truncation or lowering to satisfy budget |
| Hyperparameters | Accepted baseline experiment policy/registered supported defaults recorded before outcome | No adaptive tuning, alternate seeds or extra steps after seeing the score |
| Step count | Actual LogicalDpoTrainingUnit / DpoTrainer API and optimizer counter behavior | The default alias was inspected but its numeric value was not: resolve it. Enforce one actual increment, not one vague RunAsync call |
| Runtime/command | Existing approved one-lesson caller; if absent, S04.08 creates and tests a thin one | Do not run the 50-example routing runner or generic TP1 eval CLI as the experiment |
| Local artifact paths | Existing layout factories and session owner | No guessed directory, arbitrary caller output root, or new full checkout |

Select the first eligible teaching-only input by the frozen existing priority/ordering; once selected it is immutable. If baseline doesn't establish a usable weakness and no approved selector covers it, do not fabricate a task from closed data. Internal engineering can add the missing allowed selector over approved categories, but new content rights cannot be invented.

## 2. Identity record
Bind objective/grant ID, run ID, source candidate and relevant input digest, runtime/platform, parent weights/manifest/optimizer/RNG identities, tokenizer/model/LoRA identity, S03 contract/noise policy, corpus manifest/case-set digest, teacher/evaluator profile+model digests, teaching-source identity, eventual pair and dataset hashes, single-step limit, actual canonical artifact addresses and limits. Record local path resolution without echoing private content or secrets to remote logs.

The run ID is stable across resume. Choose it once through the owning session's create-new operation and store it before calls. An objective lookup finds an existing attempt before allocating a new one. Parent/pair/evaluator changes cannot silently create a new identity to bypass prior side effects. JSON generated in `.kiro/work/` is an evidence pointer, not a bearer grant the production API trusts.

## 3. Baseline before pair — important reconciliation
At the inspected source, TrainAndPublishAsync takes a sealed DPO receipt before it calls CalibrateBaselineAsync. The active program instead uses baseline sufficiency/weakness to decide whether a new pair should be authored. Do not resolve this by:
- authoring an unnecessary pair first;
- measuring before authoring and then measuring a second baseline in the coordinator;
- passing an arbitrary score/report from an untrusted CLI object;
- constructing 50 fake routing records to satisfy a bulk runner.

S04.03 must inspect the current post-S03 owner. Reuse an existing trusted prepared-assessment lifecycle if present. Otherwise implement the smallest typed prepared-baseline continuation: generated by the real assessment service, saved/reopened under a trusted session, bound to the accepted parent/exam/conditions and actual receipts, and consumed by the coordinator only after exact validation. Once the pair is sealed, the baseline-attempt journal contains that SAME original assessment plus pair identity and is reread before mutation. The preparation record and journal have distinct lifecycle purposes; they must not hold conflicting baseline values. Never claim the proposed type exists before implementation.

## 4. One live process versus task prompts
S04.09 can prepare baseline; S04.10 can seal the pair. S04.11 invokes the actual full coordinator/driver which may update, assess and locally publish in one transaction. S04.12 reads/completes those results without another call if already complete. Do not split a safety-critical transaction merely to fit the task numbering.

The operational surface established in S04.08 can support inspect/prepare/author/execute-or-resume/report semantics. Those names are a PROPOSED interface, not executable flags in the existing code. Freeze the exact project/entrypoint, arguments, exit codes and side effects after reading the real implementation; test help/dry-run before live invocation. A dry-run must not call models, claim a training attempt or mutate weights.

## 5. Finite effect and resource accounting
Record dispatched versus completed calls separately; ambiguous HTTP timeout is a consumed/uncertain call, not 'zero'. Record N*R per complete baseline/trial, cumulative incomplete attempts, teacher requests, runtime elapsed and CI budget separately. Frozen evaluation settings never change because retry resources are low. Check free disk and required peak allocations from actual existing checkpoint/state sizes plus temporary atomic writes and reference copy requirements. Do not claim an arbitrary multiplier is a measured memory profile; use conservative source-based bounds and available host telemetry.

Before mutation all output/rollback storage is available, the live candidate is frozen, no other writer/model-update operation owns this lineage, and the full post-update bundle can be durably validated. If a local service restart is safe and admitted, perform it normally once; do not stop another user's process or change networking/security.

## File: PROPOSED-CONTRACTS.md

# Minimal proposed contracts — not an invented existing implementation

The actual inspected owners are indexed in context-manifest.json. Resolve post-S03 equivalents first. These names are design labels until the architect pins exact types/signatures and a real compiler verifies them. The pack supplies no fake production code or placeholder runnable command.

## A. One-lesson application/session owner
Prefer an existing CodeDocumentation application coordinator under 26.TrainableRouter/Training. It owns experiment/run identity, sequencing and references to durable effects. It composes existing lower-level corpus, teacher-admission, dataset, baseline journal, DPO executor, quality gate and model publication boundaries. Lower-level DPO/TrainingLoop must not reference TrainableRouter or ContinuousLearning solely to persist a session.

A narrow session record distinguishes discovered/prepared baseline, sealed pair, committed update, complete trial decision and selected accepted state. No user-controlled `authorized=true`, `clean=true`, `score=...` or arbitrary filesystem path grants execution. Bind the owner-adopted scope through the existing server/session admission mechanism; values from work reports are independently reopened/validated.

Each transition requires its durable predecessors and uses the existing single-writer/idempotency machinery. Missing evidence means an unknown transition, not permission to start again. The session is an operational record, not a new general-purpose workflow engine, event-sourcing library or authority-intake hierarchy.

## B. Prepared baseline before teacher authoring
The assessment owner produces immutable calibrated data with accepted-parent, exam/generation/evaluator/noise identities and request receipts. Its durable prepared record is reread before the teaching decision. After the pair exists, the normal baseline-attempt journal binds those exact values to the pair before weights change. Public caller-provided scores are never trusted.

Validate preflight before evaluator calls where possible and again against actual receipts after calls. Do not require baseline/trial model hashes to be equal: model change is what is measured. Do require the parent/prepared-baseline identity to match before the new update.

## C. Complete outcome for negative as well as positive quality
The existing EvaluateTrialAsync path returns detailed evidence for accepted improvement but a generic NotImproved error for other valid comparisons in the inspected window. Reconcile current S03 changes. Where still necessary, expose/persist a typed COMPLETE measurement disposition for improved, regressed, indistinguishable or identical-weights outcomes while preserving the original nonpromoting/error-facing API semantics.

Do not convert malformed evaluator output, missing receipt, unequal conditions, cancellation or incomplete repetitions into a valid unchanged outcome. Persist actual measurement before rejecting promotion, retaining its case/repeat/request identity, mean, relevant noise floors, difference and source model IDs. An optional diagnostic string copied from an exception is not equivalent to measured evidence.

## D. Operational driver, not replacement trainer
Reuse an existing narrow server/CLI entrypoint if it composes the actual one-lesson route. Otherwise one small project or command handler may be added at the existing tools/host layer after internal plan review. It validates the prepared run contract, calls existing application methods, and returns structured results/exit status. Side effects are off by default and require the adopted bound session grant; the manager may supply the explicit execution option automatically after the code preflight passes, without asking the user again.

The actual command is recorded in the admitted run packet after build/help tests, not guessed here. A demo wrapper with scripted evaluator/model output cannot be the real mode. Test-only dependencies remain inaccessible from real composition, including observer/forced-failure hooks.

## E. Final claim model
Keep independent dimensions:
- engineering_verified;
- real_lesson_status = not_run / incomplete / completed / no_training_needed;
- real_update_count;
- quality_outcome = improved / regressed / indistinguishable / identical_weights / invalid_or_incomplete;
- accepted_parent_or_child identity;
- local_artifacts_verified;
- final_review_verdict;
- remote_artifacts_status (S05, not claimed here).

`no_training_needed` carries actual baseline evidence and no pair/update; it does not satisfy a literal 'one update executed' claim. Completed regression is a legitimate experiment outcome but never an accepted improved model. A changed weight hash alone is not evidence of improved semantics.

This index is not the skills themselves. Five principal kickoff ranges were actually read at the pinned revision; the whole library was not read here. Each fresh role reads its applicable current sections and required dependencies. Existing routing wins for actual file location; unresolved paths are reported and resolved rather than silently skipped.

| Skill | Actual path | Inspected kickoff range |

| architecture | `docs/skills/trainable-architecture-decision-SKILL.md` | 1–115 |

| code_review | `docs/skills/generated-code-review-SKILL.md` | 1–110 |
| human_language | `docs/skills/output-readability-gate-SKILL.md` | 1–125 |

## Additional routing seeds
- `docs/SESSION-START-PROMPT.md` — Current work-order and delegation contract; file is under docs/, not root.
- `docs/skills/planning-session-startup-SKILL.md` — Resolve actual SK-SESSION/INTENT/PLAN/REVIEW/ARCH routes.
- `docs/skills/architect-behavior-classifier-SKILL.md` — Separate learned model behavior from deterministic routing/storage guards.
- `docs/skills/authority-requirement-binding-SKILL.md` — Bind to this adopted source requirement, not an archived command.
- `docs/skills/reference-inventory-and-source-map-SKILL.md` — Refresh code locations and source identity; do not invent references.
- `docs/skills/no-product-decisions-SKILL.md` — Preserve approved model/data/evaluation policy; ordinary implementation naming is internal.
- `docs/skills/claim-verification-SKILL.md` — Separate static, executed synthetic, real lesson and certification claims.

The manager resolves source-reading order and gives each worker the smallest sufficient packet: its task, its role, the accepted internal plan, current source windows, expected observations and relevant previous result. Do not omit an applicable mandatory dependency to fit a made-up token budget. Do not read the entire all-tasks file into every child. Read complete edited files and changed call paths, preserving actual safety and test requirements.

Architect: ownership, exact trusted input/output, sequencing, recovery and layer boundaries. Plan reviewer: source completeness, resource/authority inputs, invariants and test falsifiers. Executor: real APIs, correct .NET types, mutation ownership, actual tests and runtime receipts. Code/evidence reviewer: actual diff, callers, branch reachability, raw results, side-effect count and truthful final claims. Human-language skill applies to all reports; user-facing explanation precedes internal identifiers.

The owner-adopted START/AUTHORIZATION extend S03 v2's Luna/xhigh and requirement-based review method. A 30/50-pass legacy quota is not silently ignored: this explicit task-scoped override replaces that operating choice while preserving required coverage and independence. Never label one author's self-check as a separate review. A worker may request Luna/xhigh without telemetry proving it; keep that distinction and do not waste work probing text for model identity.

Official Kiro effort reference, checked during preparation: https://kiro.dev/docs/models/effort/
The inspected documentation lists Luna `xhigh`; IDE/CLI expose effort selection. Set it in native controls, not just this text. No Luna benchmark or measured optimal context size was performed for this pack.

## File: TESTING.md

# Testing S04 — four different kinds of evidence

## 1. Source/contract review
This verifies the call graph, trust boundaries, ordering, real vs proposed API, current required skills and exact operation semantics. It is not a test run. Every task's checks table has the required observation and falsifier. Read complete changed files/methods, not only the source-map window.

## 2. Synthetic component and integration tests
Use the current `Tests/LlmFromScratch.Tests.csproj`, its actual SDK/target framework/VSTest configuration and established lane. `gate-plan.json` is an additive proposal; resolve existing/newly compiled class/method names before adding a gate to the installed runner. Do not treat proposed tests as already present or run a nonmatching filter to obtain exit0.

Intended native shape, conditional on the admitted current runner and selected configuration:
```
dotnet restore Tests/LlmFromScratch.Tests.csproj -p:EnableWindowsTargeting=true
dotnet build Tests/LlmFromScratch.Tests.csproj -c Release --no-restore -p:EnableWindowsTargeting=true
dotnet test Tests/LlmFromScratch.Tests.csproj -c Release --no-restore --no-build -p:EnableWindowsTargeting=true --filter "FullyQualifiedName~CodeDocumentationRepositoryOwnedDpoTrainingPublicationCoordinatorTests" --logger "trx;LogFileName=coordinator.trx" --results-directory <actual-runner-owned-results>
```
The last path is not a literal argument to paste. The admitted supervisor supplies a unique output directory outside scratch. Replace the illustrative class filter with the resolved task mapping, not an invented name. Rebuild after source changes before `--no-build`. Add the property consistently where required; it does not make Windows-specific behavior verifiable on Linux.

Rehearsals use actual model arithmetic, storage, lease/identity guard and coordinator as appropriate, with scripted transport only in test composition. Do not put test factories, forced failures or chosen pass values into real mode. Required negative tests need a positive route-reachability control. No early platform return can prove a platform-specific contract. Existing accepted controls can be reused if code/input equivalence is recorded; never report them as fresh executions.

## 3. Real one-lesson evidence
Only S04.09–13 under the adopted grant produce real scientific evidence. Native CI/TRX success does not satisfy this. Required chain:
accepted parent + compatible complete state -> original real baseline saved/read -> teaching-only real pair sealed/read -> one actual update -> same real trial evaluation -> honest disposition -> selected state reopened.

A valid baseline-sufficient branch has zero pair/update and a distinct `no_training_needed` result. A real failed/incomplete invocation is not completed unchanged. A complete negative comparison can be scientifically useful and accepted as a correctly executed experiment. No actual model improvement is promised.

## 4. Separate final review
Reviewer verifies source/candidate/workflow/actual runtime identities, test definitions and outcomes, all real receipts/artifact hashes and the policy arithmetic. It is a separate context from the author. Review cannot convert missing runtime evidence into PASS or invent a model/effort observation.

## TRX/evidence validation
Require parseable, bounded XML with the expected structure; prohibit external entity resolution. Match each result ID to one test definition/method and the admitted class/namespace; check exactly the required test cases, including parameterized rows where applicable. Require numeric nonnegative totals; nonzero execution; all required cases Passed; no required skips/aborts; result counts consistent; malformed/duplicate/missing definitions fail. Read the real writer's counter schema: never require a nonexistent skipped attribute or infer all required methods from class total alone. A complete failed run remains failed even with a valid XML file.

When focused and impacted are identical and no experiment requirement demands repetition, one candidate-matched run may satisfy both mappings; report overlap explicitly, not double unique coverage or unnecessary billable repeats. When a broader caller/helper changes, select its actual relevant existing suite; do not ignore failing regression tests as unrelated without causal analysis and an explicit scope disposition.

## Required evidence per run
Actual repo/source revision + local candidate digest; current compiled inputs and assembly identities; workflow/ref/run/job/attempt or local PID and command; model/data identity where relevant; configured vs observed SDK/platform; numeric stage exits; discovered/executed/passed/failed/not-executed/aborted and required-method outcomes; raw result paths; closed-data exposure category; separate reviewer identity/verdict. Record what was reused versus run afresh. Report-only commits must not relabel the tested source SHA.

## Resource cleanup
Clean only current run-owned temporary fixtures and processes under the exact recorded ownership. Retain baseline/pair/trial/model-state/evaluation artifacts as durable real results, including negative outcomes. Historical old16/new12 and unrelated pending S02 work remain untouched. Missing cleanup is reported separately and never hidden with a test PASS; no broad git clean/reset/quarantine.

## File: RECOVERY-PLAYBOOK.md

# Recovery by durable effect — not by last command exit

This is the proposed S04 operational decision table. Reconcile each row with the actual admitted session/attempt implementation before real release. Synthetic tests in S04.07 must demonstrate the assigned action. Runtime report text is never the authority for whether an optimizer update happened.

| Last proven durable condition | Safe next action within the same objective | Evidence required | Never |
|---|---|---|---|
| No session/call/update, clean inputs | Create one session using existing exclusive owner, then prepare | Objective lookup and create-new/lease result | Allocate new objective just because earlier work report missing |
| Baseline call dispatched, result incomplete | Record consumed/uncertain request, keep incomplete series; at most one allowed complete replacement before pair/update | Actual request receipts, policy unchanged, remaining cumulative allowance | Pretend no call happened or average successful subset |
| Complete baseline saved, pair absent | Reopen baseline; run existing sufficiency decision; author only if needed | Same parent/exam/conditions and trusted prepared record | Recalibrate baseline for a new worker |
| Baseline sufficient | Persist no_training_needed and zero-effect result; final review and certification handoff | Actual complete baseline and exact predicate result | Train anyway to satisfy a desired task count or claim a certificate |
| Teacher call dispatched, no durable valid pair | Reconcile response/store; bounded retry only for protocol/transient failure within cap | Actual receipt/partial persistence/ownership | Generate many candidates and score them on the closed exam |
| One valid pair sealed, update not started | Reopen pair+baseline+pre-state; proceed once | Pair novelty and trusted zero-update state | Reauthor or change ID/text |
| Attempt claimed, trainer provably not reached | Fix the diagnosed prerequisite and use existing supported same-objective continuation | Production state proving zero optimizer effect and unambiguous lease ownership | Delete claim to force fresh path |
| Optimizer invocation dispatched, commit status unknown | Inspect actual durable bundle/rollback state; prohibit new optimizer calls until resolved | State-family/step/commit evidence, not process exit alone | Assume failed command means no weight change |
| Complete post-step bundle saved, evaluation incomplete | Validate original baseline/pair/trial/optimizer/RNG, then trial-only resume within call cap | Hash/length/dimensions/step/topology and current identity checks | Train the pair again, choose new baseline or mutate exam |
| Bundle missing/corrupt/mixed after possible update | Mark recovery_required; preserve parent and partial data; bounded source/fixture diagnosis | Exact failed file/identity and update uncertainty | Reset RNG/optimizer, reseed, or reconstruct fake commit metadata |
| Complete valid negative comparison | Verify exact parent retention/rehydration; return same outcome | Full calibrated result, policy and selected-state identity | Repeat until a positive score appears |
| Complete improvement, publication not confirmed | Reopen lineage/artifacts and apply existing idempotent publication completion only if admitted | Original decision and matching parent/trial/child identities | Reevaluate or create another training step |
| Accepted child exists, report missing | Reconstruct safe report from existing validated evidence | Parent/pair/child/decision chain | Use duplicate-pair error as permission to train a new ID |
| Requested cancellation before mutation | Stop cleanly and preserve preparation as owned durable evidence | Zero update state and cancelled call accounting | Mark the lesson completed |
| Cancellation after mutation | Retain/validate bundle, restore safe selected state or resume evaluation | Exact observed effects and policy | Treat cancellation as zero effect automatically |
| Source/config/corpus drift | Freeze real actions and classify compatibility; fix code offline where safe | Explicit old/new candidate+input difference and bound contract | Relabel old measurements with new SHA or restart experiment budget |

## Retry limits are not statistical selection rules
Engineering CI retries test code and can follow corrective commits. Model authoring retries are limited to invalid/transport outcomes before sealing. Evaluation retries only replace an actually incomplete/invalid series, preserve all prior requests and use exactly the original contract. A complete series stays in evidence even when it fails quality. No partial-series splicing or score-based stopping. Training retries need proof that no real optimizer effect has occurred; an uncertain effect is never treated as zero.

## Ownership and cleanup
Real baseline, pair, trial, state and decision are durable scientific evidence, including negative outcomes. Test/driver temporary buffers/directories are disposable only with established ownership and exact path. Existing secure file checks, denied operations and unknown historical ownership remain. Logical 'quarantine' labels do not authorize creating quarantine directories/copies or deleting evidence.

## Safe task continuation
A missing real-host input may block live tasks while code/tests/review proceed. An unaccepted evaluator identity contract blocks the real comparison and downstream acceptance, not harmless independent documentation. Never convert a hidden dependency into a PASS to avoid asking the user; resolve ordinary source/CI issues internally and name the smallest genuinely external missing action once.

## File: CI-PLAYBOOK.md

# CI and local-runtime playbook inherited from the autonomous S03 method

Use the current working pipeline/runner and published candidate, not a new execution platform. The initial historical run IDs and local SAC failures are evidence, not commands to wait forever or repeat a known denial. Manager operations need no new human approval inside AUTHORIZATION.md.

## Before dispatch
Read actual workflow, run/jobs, Actions permissions, selected action policy, image/SDK, current candidate and existing spending/reservations. Preserve repository/organization security and minimal job permissions. Reuse current authorized GitHub-owned actions, versions/pinning rules and signed policy requirements. A 401/403/account block is not overcome by changing token/shell. Do not install a self-hosted runner on a blocked machine as a workaround.

The existing S03 Windows workflow may hard-code the S03.01 eight methods/filter. Running it unchanged cannot prove S04. Reconcile the current runner gate mapping, then add the exact admitted S04 filters and required methods to the existing mechanism. If a thin generalization is needed, review its delta, allowlist typed inputs and avoid shell interpolation of arbitrary expressions. Keep S03 obligations unchanged; do not replace the entire runner or write a new evidence framework.

For manual workflow registration, use the current supported GitHub process and default-branch registration requirement. A source-only candidate may be committed/pushed before its tests: candidate publication is not acceptance. Main CI-only PRs may be merged under protections without including the unaccepted product backlog. Keep actual workflow revision/input candidate/checked-out source identities separate.

## Queue and run state
| Observed state | Action |
|---|---|
| Matching running job with progress | Observe it; no duplicate dispatch. |
| Matching completed results | Validate results; do not rerun unchanged tests solely for activity. |
| Queued, jobs0, no progress15min after actual prerequisites enabled | Check service/concurrency/approvals/budget messages once; cancel stale own run, confirm state, then replacement if no real denial. Previously elapsed confirmed time counts. |
| Ordinary cancellation fails to finish | Re-read, then one documented force-cancel; accepted request is not terminal-state proof. |
| 409 but still queued/jobs0 | Within budget and absent access/payment denial, one replacement may be made while reserving cost for both potential runs. Cancel late duplicate if it starts. |
| Replacement also queues | At most two replacements for one unchanged scheduling failure. Diagnose actual platform/account constraint or use another already admitted compatible CI image, not an unlimited queue. |
| Dispatch response ambiguous | Search run identity before another POST; record invocation ID/branch/actor/time/candidate. |
| Waiting for approval | Perform allowed own approval using existing rights, never impersonate or bypass another required approver. |
| Infrastructure transient with unchanged source | Bounded retry/backoff, preserve failure evidence. |
| Source/workflow corrected | Commit corrected candidate and dispatch that revision. Old-event rerun does not validate newly edited source. |

15 minutes/two replacements are operational thresholds, not GitHub guarantees. No money is reset by retry or task migration. Unknown included minutes are not a repeated owner question: reserve conservatively from the same remaining shared USD20 cap, with current official rates and storage costs. Standard runners only. Local real model work must not be silently substituted by hosted test fixtures or private data upload.

## Failure classification
Build error -> inspect first real compiler diagnostic, actual type/method source, then fix/rebuild. ModelConfig is the observed model configuration type; never guess LlmConfig.
Zero discovered/executed tests -> fix assembly/filter/discovery; no PASS from exit0.
Test assertion failure -> reached production boundary and fixture diagnosis, not copy actual into expected.
TRX validator failure -> distinguish evidence schema defect from failing tests. Standard VSTest uses `notExecuted`, not a mandatory `skipped` counter. Read actual supported schema; require all necessary nonnegative counts, outcomes, definitions and methods, not default missing values to zero.
Timeout -> inspect actual progress/owned process; fix deadlock or split unchanged test coverage with explicit union, not omit cases.
Flaky result -> diagnose state/isolation and show repair; one lucky rerun is not proof.
Artifact unavailable -> inspect failure logs/result generation and retained raw evidence, not fabricate a report.

Do not run local denied test assemblies unchanged, change SAC/WDAC/Defender/registry or purchase certificates. Existing local permitted application preflight is separate from CI. If no private real-training route works, keep live completion blocked and do useful engineering; a cloud test run cannot manufacture a local lesson.

## File: DECISION-EXAMPLES.md

# Worked examples — illustrative values, never runtime evidence

## Valid scientific outcomes
| Example | Recorded illustrative means | Noise floors | Correct disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Clear positive change | baseline0.60, trial0.70, difference+0.10 | baseline0.02, trial0.03 | Improvement exceeds both floors; only otherwise-valid matching evidence can authorize local child promotion. |
| Positive point estimate, inconclusive | 0.60 -> 0.62, difference+0.02 | 0.02 and0.03 | Not proven beyond both; keep original state. Do not round or remeasure until green. |
| Only one floor exceeded | 0.60 -> 0.64, difference+0.04 | 0.01 and0.05 | Not proven improvement under both; unchanged selection. |
| Clear regression | 0.60 -> 0.54, difference-0.06 | 0.02 and0.03 | Keep/reopen exact original state and record regression. Correct experiment execution need not produce a better model. |
| Same weights | same trusted policy hash before/after | any valid comparable series | Do not claim learning from a changed timestamp or loss log. Record counter/effect facts and identical-weight/retention outcome according to real policy. |
| Malformed evaluator | no valid complete trial mean | not applicable | Invalid/incomplete, not 'unchanged' and never a synthetic score0. |

Apply actual current MeasurementNoisePolicy at runtime. These rows explain decisions, not a new formula or calibrated data. A .95 target in source is not automatically a 95% confidence statement. The exact boundary predicate may require positive margin beyond noise, not simple score>=.95.

## Call budgeting example
For N=5 fixed diagnostic cases and R=5 existing repeats, one complete baseline has25 evaluation responses and one trial has25. Fresh comparison therefore contains50 valid responses. A failed partial series does not become another set of valid cases: its actual dispatched requests remain in cumulative accounting and any allowed full replacement uses the original contract. The cap4*N*R=100 is a proposed cumulative operational ceiling, not permission to obtain four favorable valid scores or to exceed a tighter configured client budget.

## Task prompts are not runtime side-effect boundaries
Correct: prepare baseline through actual driver; author one pair; invoke coordinator once, which itself trains, assesses and publishes if valid; the following task reads that complete decision.
Incorrect: S04.11 trains through TrainAndPublishAsync, then S04.12 calls TrainAndPublishAsync again with a new run ID because its title says evaluate.

## Lossy negative result
Correct: a complete regression measurement is persisted with actual baseline/trial identities, means, noise and request receipts; the existing API still refuses model promotion; the driver reports completed_regressed.
Incorrect: catch any NotImproved/Error and construct a successful report with fabricated equal scores. Incompatible evaluation and missing model state remain failures, not scientific unchanged outcomes.

## Operational retry is not learning retry
Correct: YAML compilation fails before any job; fix YAML, publish candidate and dispatch within CI budget.
Correct: real complete trial bundle exists, evaluator transport incomplete; validate all identities and resume permitted trial-only measurement.
Incorrect: real command timed out after optimizer may have run; allocate a new TrainingRunId and try training again.

## Privacy
Correct: the approved isolated evaluator internally sees a closed reference; the teaching model sees a separate teaching method/reference; the agent report carries permitted aggregate metrics and hashes.
Incorrect: closed reference is pasted into Luna to explain an error, or real model/pair/assessment bytes are uploaded to GitHub under a generic 'all evidence' artifact path.

Read this full task plus the appropriate dispatch and current adopted AUTHORIZATION/OPERATING-POLICY. A task catalogue entry is not proof that work is missing or accepted.

## 1. Task prompt, acceptance and bounded scope

**CURRENT TASK:** Determine the earliest real S04 gap and bind one objective to one compatible accepted model and the existing S01–S03 contracts.

**CURRENT SOURCE / EVIDENCE:** The queue is conditional. The inspected head is a newer code snapshot, not proof that all S03 is accepted. The current coordinator already has one-pair and recovery guards; the routing runner performs a different 50-example profile task.

**Do the following; resolve ordinary corrections internally:**

1. Read the uploaded queue S04/S05 and active training-program opening. Recheck actual HEAD, branch, local modifications and the newest S02/S03 evidence. Preserve existing real-run/attempt and pending evidence instead of resetting state.

2. Produce one bounded mapping: source requirement -> real production owner/caller -> existing assertion/result -> actual uncovered behavior -> this pack task. Scope the work to the currently approved CodeDocumentation domain/unit. If an existing S04 attempt/result is found, reconcile it before allocating any new objective.

3. Identify the original accepted-model target through configured ownership, not by newest filename or best observed score. Record the explicit one-real-lesson grant source and remaining shared CI budget. Do not derive live authority from an old quoted command.

4. Resolve four known integration questions internally: baseline before pair without recalibration; detailed negative quality evidence; actual one-step semantics; real operational entrypoint. Reuse current equivalents; proposed contracts do not prove gaps exist.

5. Distinguish blocked upstream functionality from old cleanup holds. Complete independent source work while collecting the exact prerequisite evidence needed before a live call. If a genuine S03 contract is missing, return its narrow fix to the existing S03 task instead of expanding S04 silently.

6. Assign S04.01 next or the nearest unmet prerequisite. No whole-repository inventory, new arbitrary 50-pass audit or another authority-intake phase.

**Acceptance:**
- Every R01–R15 row has an owning task and current-vs-proposed status.
- Real execution is not released solely because documents exist.
- A unique objective or explicit unresolved target is recorded; no model mutation, teacher call or duplicate implementation occurred.

**Default write/operation scope; exact source delta is internally admitted:**

**Not the task:** no earlier-slice restart, no arbitrary model/provider/data change, no extra learning update, no closed-data disclosure, no weakening tests, no remote real-artifact publication and no broad governance/runner rewrite. Engineering tests are synthetic evidence only. Live stages use the same bounded objective.

## 4. Positive examples

| Situation | Correct behavior |

| An earlier S03 identity test already ran on matching inputs | Reuse its assertions and result identity; map the next missing contract instead of adding an identical class. |

These are examples of the intended behavior, not recorded execution results. Use real source/receipt observations when claiming that this task passed.

## 5. Negative examples and why they fail

| Tempting shortcut | Why it violates the contract |
|---|---|
| Set s03_accepted=true to unblock S04 | A status edit cannot establish the independent evaluation contract. |
| Run the 50/50 router and call its score the saved-model baseline | It proves a different routing-profile task and may bulk-author pairs. |
| Start a fresh run because a previous attempt report is incomplete | Durable effect evidence must be reconciled first; a report gap is not zero prior training. |

## 6. How to test or verify

| Check | Arrange / action | Required observable result |
|---|---|---|
| Source reconciliation | Read actual entrypoints and the supplied queue; record exact ranges/revisions | S04 means one real saved-model lesson, not another slice number. |
| Duplicate-work control | Locate accepted equivalent tasks/tests and matching result references | Equivalent work is retained; missing evidence is labelled missing evidence. |
| Authority readiness | Compare current startup adoption and live restrictions | One operative bounded grant; no repeated approval demand for its ordinary operations. |
| Task graph | Check every dependency and requirements map | No cycle; no live stage bypasses S01–S03 functional checks. |
| Artifact integrity | Read back changed report and parse any edited state JSON; diff-check | Only planning changes; history and existing source preserved. |

This task does not get a fabricated .NET filter. Planning/readiness uses scoped artifact/preflight checks; live tasks require actual model/runtime receipts; final review consumes their actual evidence. Any necessary code correction returns to its engineering task and gate.

Use TESTING.md and the actual admitted runner. Build after source edits before `--no-build`. Required test execution is nonzero with exact expected methods/cases and no unreported platform-return/skip/abort. Keep original raw results and numeric exits. Reuse unchanged evidence only with verified relevant input identity. Live measurements are never certified by a scripted test result or class total. Counts from overlapping focused/impacted runs are not added as unique coverage.

## 7. Roles, orchestration and automatic continuation

| Role | Responsibility | Dispatch |
|---|---|---|
| architect | Resolve current scope and the four integration questions, selecting equivalent owners before proposals. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.00/architect.md` |
| plan-reviewer | Check fidelity to queue S04 and reject hidden missing inputs or assumptions of upstream acceptance. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.00/plan-reviewer.md` |
| executor | Write the source-bound admission packet only; do not run .NET or real model work for this planning task. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.00/executor.md` |
| code-reviewer | Review actual mapping and state changes as artifacts, not a product-code PASS. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.00/code-reviewer.md` |

Use one genuine source writer or live-effect owner at a time. Architect and reviewers do not edit product source or operate live model effects. Each may append its own attributed task-report section; no fabricated worker verdict. Executor alone owns admitted source/runtime actions. The manager handles current state, selected source publication and routine CI. Ordinary allowed corrections cycle internally, no new question to the user.

For a live stage, reuse S04.08's admitted runtime plan and check only actual deltas; this is not another human approval. If the compound production call already produced the next stage's output, pass it forward rather than repeat effects. After acceptance select the next real dependency-ready task; a commit or static PASS does not terminate the objective. Worker failure and genuine external blockers follow OPERATING-POLICY.md.

## 8. Edge cases, permitted response and return packet

| Edge case | Required response without routine owner interruption |
|---|---|
| Multiple active CodeDocumentation targets | Use configured authoritative selector; if none resolves the conflict, report only that missing target and finish other preparation. |
| S03 still being edited | One writer at a time; do not modify or freeze its APIs until returned candidate is stable. |
| Only narrative PASS exists | Find raw evidence/current input mapping; mark verification unestablished until it exists. |
| Queue file locally changed | Read both supplied requirement and current binding; preserve stronger supported constraints without blindly overwriting. |
| A previous S04 completed negatively | Do not generate another pair to force success; verify that objective and complete final reporting. |

Use RECOVERY-PLAYBOOK.md for last-proven-durable-state decisions, CI-PLAYBOOK.md for scheduling/build/tests, and AUTHORIZATION.md for limits. These are distinct: a CI retry is not permission for another learning update. Do not resolve a hard denial by another shell/loader/token. Unknown artifact ownership is not an invitation to delete.

**Return packet:** explain the human result first, then actual role/model telemetry, grant reference, source/skill reads, requirement-to-owner-to-assertion/effect mapping, changed files/candidate identity, commands/exits/raw result paths, actual update/provider-call counts if any, selected-state/quality disposition, precise review verdict and the next permitted action. Missing evidence remains missing; no real improvement, process restart or remote publication is inferred. Report one narrow external action only when it genuinely cannot be performed under the adopted grant.

# S04.01 — Resolve real model, data, runtime and budgets up front

**Requested worker:** GPT-5.6 Luna · `xhigh`

**Kind:** readiness · **Goal:** Prove that a policy-permitted real runtime and the approved local assets exist before creating any lesson or spending evaluation calls.

**Source pins:** target `15f2f2cb905f3e8db37103bc032678b789071f6e`; planning `f8a0bde3c596422f349bf7b87093394c8a7bdc44`. Pins are references, never reset targets.

**Dependencies:** S04.00. **Branch condition:** all applicable predecessor contracts must be established.

Read this full task plus the appropriate dispatch and current adopted AUTHORIZATION/OPERATING-POLICY. A task catalogue entry is not proof that work is missing or accepted.

## 1. Task prompt, acceptance and bounded scope

**CURRENT TASK:** Prove that a policy-permitted real runtime and the approved local assets exist before creating any lesson or spending evaluation calls.

**CURRENT SOURCE / EVIDENCE:** The start service validates accepted repository weights and compatibility. CI success does not establish access to local Qwen, tokenizer, private teaching references or model files. Historical SAC failures concern their exact blocked artifacts, not every possible command.

**Do the following; resolve ordinary corrections internally:**

1. Resolve the concrete parent/tree and dataset/corpus locators from the current approved configuration. Read bounded metadata and identities; use the existing loader validator where safely admitted. An LFS pointer or corrupt checkpoint is not a loaded parent.

2. Resolve real local authoring/evaluation model IDs, profile IDs, manifest/layer digests, provider endpoint and existing license/deployment evidence. A ready factory definition is not a reachable running service. Read installed configuration/status through permitted methods without sending exam prompts or logging secrets.

3. Resolve the real runtime executable/module identity and supported .NET lane, with prior SAC/tool denial scope. Prefer the already accepted runtime; do not disable security, reuse a known blocked test DLL, or silently move real assets into hosted CI. A normal preflight of a different admitted application may establish that it works, but not as a disguise for the denied artifact.

4. Check required free disk, expected largest buffers/checkpoints, existing model residency, cancellation and elapsed-time limits. Do not offload/quantize or replace the accepted student/teacher to make memory fit. Configure sequential use of existing local teacher/evaluator resources where the established implementation supports it.

5. Freeze N, R, generation settings, evaluation/noise contract and derived per-series/cumulative call allowances in the existing S04 run record. Use 4*N*R only as an upper operational ceiling; obey tighter configured admission limits. The intended successful fresh comparison requires exactly 2*N*R evaluation calls.

6. Resolve existing startup/CLI or service surface. If none handles the one-lesson flow, assign the thin entrypoint to S04.08. Missing API wiring is an engineering task, not a request for a new owner/provider. Missing actual private data or permitted compute is a concrete external prerequisite.

7. Return an asset/runtime readiness report. Resolve ordinary configuration defects within authorized existing inputs; do not leave predictable model/host availability discovery until S04.11.

**Acceptance:**
- Exact accepted parent/tokenizer/config and local teacher/evaluator identities are known, or each genuinely missing item is named.
- No real assessment/authoring/update is performed in this task.
- The driver/runtime plan and resource budget are feasible or explicitly blocked without a false claim that CI proves local readiness.

**Default write/operation scope; exact source delta is internally admitted:**
- Read model/config metadata and existing permitted runtime status; no model/data mutation.
- Minimal non-secret operational configuration may be corrected after internal plan review using existing approved values; record its exact path. No new provider, subscription or broad network/security changes.

**Not the task:** no earlier-slice restart, no arbitrary model/provider/data change, no extra learning update, no closed-data disclosure, no weakening tests, no remote real-artifact publication and no broad governance/runner rewrite. Engineering tests are synthetic evidence only. Live stages use the same bounded objective.

Requirement bindings: R02, R03, R06, R09, R13, R14. One role-attributed report: `.kiro/xiigen-slices/work/S04/S04.01.md`; manager alone integrates live active/state. Preserve existing contents. The runtime production session and work report are different authorities.

## 4. Positive examples

| Situation | Correct behavior |
|---|---|
| Parent path points to valid sealed repository bytes with matching tokenizer | Record its lineage and hashes and reserve sufficient disk for the existing transactional path. |
| Local endpoint stopped, exact installed owned service known and no other workload | Normally start the already approved service under existing user rights, then check readiness; no admin/firewall changes and no exam call. |

These are examples of the intended behavior, not recorded execution results. Use real source/receipt observations when claiming that this task passed.

## 5. Negative examples and why they fail

| Tempting shortcut | Why it violates the contract |
|---|---|
| Use a tiny random model because the accepted weights are missing | It would not be the real accepted compatible parent required by S04. |
| GitHub test run passed, therefore local Qwen is ready | These are different machines and assets. |
| Send the closed corpus to a convenient hosted provider | It changes privacy, provenance, budget and approved evaluator. |
| State MaxTeacherCalls=50 although actual plan needs100 | Never shrink the exam or misstate resource permission to pass preflight. |

## 6. How to test or verify

| Check | Arrange / action | Required observable result |
|---|---|---|
| Configuration identity | Read current selected target and approved profile digests | Every role/asset maps to an actual source; no mutable model label used alone. |
| Artifact validity | Use permitted bounded loader/manifest checks | Hash/length/tokenizer/model compatibility agree; missing/corrupt bytes fail closed. |
| Host feasibility | Inspect established executable/runtime/service status and permissions | The real route is feasible independently of CI; no denied operation retried unchanged. |
| Budget arithmetic | Compute N*R, 2*N*R and cumulative retry cap with overflow bounds | Required full series fits all live limits or live admission stays blocked. |
| No effect | Compare model/data identities and call/attempt records before/after preflight | No authoring, evaluator scoring or optimizer update. |

This task does not get a fabricated .NET filter. Planning/readiness uses scoped artifact/preflight checks; live tasks require actual model/runtime receipts; final review consumes their actual evidence. Any necessary code correction returns to its engineering task and gate.

Use TESTING.md and the actual admitted runner. Build after source edits before `--no-build`. Required test execution is nonzero with exact expected methods/cases and no unreported platform-return/skip/abort. Keep original raw results and numeric exits. Reuse unchanged evidence only with verified relevant input identity. Live measurements are never certified by a scripted test result or class total. Counts from overlapping focused/impacted runs are not added as unique coverage.

## 7. Roles, orchestration and automatic continuation

| Role | Responsibility | Dispatch |
|---|---|---|
| architect | Choose the existing valid asset/runtime resolution path and calculate resources without changing the scientific contract. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.01/architect.md` |
| plan-reviewer | Verify every readiness field has evidence and no anticipated host problem is hidden behind a future gate. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.01/plan-reviewer.md` |
| executor | Run only permitted metadata/runtime preflight and implement a narrow configuration correction when needed. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.01/executor.md` |
| code-reviewer | Inspect real readiness output, privacy and zero-live-effect evidence; separate source availability from executed loading. | `.kiro/xiigen-slices/luna/s04/dispatch/S04.01/code-reviewer.md` |

Use one genuine source writer or live-effect owner at a time. Architect and reviewers do not edit product source or operate live model effects. Each may append its own attributed task-report section; no fabricated worker verdict. Executor alone owns admitted source/runtime actions. The manager handles current state, selected source publication and routine CI. Ordinary allowed corrections cycle internally, no new question to the user.

For a live stage, reuse S04.08's admitted runtime plan and check only actual deltas; this is not another human approval. If the compound production call already produced the next stage's output, pass it forward rather than repeat effects. After acceptance select the next real dependency-ready task; a commit or static PASS does not terminate the objective. Worker failure and genuine external blockers follow OPERATING-POLICY.md.

## 8. Edge cases, permitted response and return packet

| Edge case | Required response without routine owner interruption |
|---|---|
| Several tags resolve to one model digest | Record canonical approved identity; never treat the name as stronger than content. |
| Model digest changed behind same tag | Do not silently accept; restore/select already approved bytes if available, else named model-identity blocker. |
| Insufficient memory or disk | Use established unload/order options for unused owned resources; no deleting historical data or altering model quality. Otherwise remain not-ready. |
| Endpoint returns401/403 | Use existing granted credentials only; no new token or alternate provider; continue offline work. |
| No signing/execution route | Report exact missing allowed host once. Do not generate pages of repeated certificate investigation. |
| No valid teaching reference | Do not ask Qwen/Luna to invent paid reference provenance; name source gap and prepare other work. |

Use RECOVERY-PLAYBOOK.md for last-proven-durable-state decisions, CI-PLAYBOOK.md for scheduling/build/tests, and AUTHORIZATION.md for limits. These are distinct: a CI retry is not permission for another learning update. Do not resolve a hard denial by another shell/loader/token. Unknown artifact ownership is not an invitation to delete.

**Return packet:** explain the human result first, then actual role/model telemetry, grant reference, source/skill reads, requirement-to-owner-to-assertion/effect mapping, changed files/candidate identity, commands/exits/raw result paths, actual update/provider-call counts if any, selected-state/quality disposition, precise review verdict and the next permitted action. Missing evidence remains missing; no real improvement, process restart or remote publication is inferred. Report one narrow external action only when it genuinely cannot be performed under the adopted grant.
